data-v-cfa7c811="" class="pill">written from the form</span><!----></div><!----><textarea data-v-cfa7c811="" class="code mono" spellcheck="false" aria-label="The Dockerfile" rows="7" value="FROM debian:12-slim@sha256:3783cc01769c7b2b1b83a5c5ad96c815348e28ed7da68e2e3687004faa906251
RUN . /etc/os-release &amp;&amp; [ &quot;$ID&quot; = debian ] &amp;&amp; [ -f /etc/apt/sources.list.d/debian.sources ] &amp;&amp; printf 'Types: deb\nURIs: http://snapshot.debian.org/archive/debian/20261006T000000Z\nSuites: %s %s-updates\nComponents: main\nSigned-By: /usr/share/keyrings/debian-archive-keyring.gpg\nCheck-Valid-Until: no\n\nTypes: deb\nURIs: http://snapshot.debian.org/archive/debian-security/20261006T000000Z\nSuites: %s-security\nComponents: main\nSigned-By: /usr/share/keyrings/debian-archive-keyring.gpg\nCheck-Valid-Until: no\n' &quot;$VERSION_CODENAME&quot; &quot;$VERSION_CODENAME&quot; &quot;$VERSION_CODENAME&quot; &gt; /etc/apt/sources.list.d/debian.sources; true
RUN apt-get update &amp;&amp; apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends gcc-arm-none-eabi libnewlib-arm-none-eabi qemu-system-arm python3 git poppler-utils &amp;&amp; rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
WORKDIR /app
COPY environment/data /app/data
" readonly=""></textarea><!----></div><div data-v-cfa7c811="" class="panel2 try"><div data-v-cfa7c811="" class="ph2"><b data-v-cfa7c811="">Try the box</b><span data-v-cfa7c811="" class="tb ok">Worked · 02:26 PM</span><!----><button data-v-cfa7c811="" class="btn sm secondary">Try again</button></div><p data-v-cfa7c811="" class="ph3">Builds the box and runs these commands, one per line.</p><!----><textarea data-v-cfa7c811="" id="try-commands" class="code mono" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Commands to run in the box" rows="15" value="ls -la /app /app/data
for f in /app/data/*; do echo &quot;===== $f&quot;; cat &quot;$f&quot;; echo; done
cat /etc/debian_version; arm-none-eabi-gcc --version | head -n1; qemu-system-arm --version | head -n1; dpkg-query -W gcc-arm-none-eabi libnewlib-arm-none-eabi qemu-system-arm
command -v python3 git pdftotext make timeout gdb-multiarch; python3 --version; true
qemu-system-arm -machine help | grep -iE 'lm3s|netduino|stm32|mps2|microbit'; true
qemu-system-arm -d help 2&gt;&amp;1 | head -n 40
qemu-system-arm -trace help 2&gt;&amp;1 | grep -E '^(sdcard_|ssi_sd|pl022|stm32f2xx|stellaris|memory_region_ops)' | head -n 40
printf '.syntax unified\n.cpu cortex-m3\n.thumb\n.text\n.word 0x20001000\n.word reset\n.thumb_func\nreset:\nldr r0,=0x400FE104\nmovs r1,#0x10\nstr r1,[r0]\nldr r0,=0x40008010\nmovs r1,#2\nstr r1,[r0]\nldr r0,=0x400FC000\nmovs r1,#1\nstr r1,[r0]\nmovs r2,#100\nloop:\nsubs r2,r2,#1\nbne loop\nldr r0,=0x40008004\nmovs r1,#2\nstr r1,[r0]\nhalt:\nwfi\nb halt\n' &gt; /tmp/lm.s &amp;&amp; arm-none-eabi-as -o /tmp/lm.o /tmp/lm.s &amp;&amp; arm-none-eabi-ld -Ttext=0 -o /tmp/lm.elf /tmp/lm.o &amp;&amp; echo lm-built
timeout 5 qemu-system-arm -M lm3s6965evb -nographic -monitor none -serial null -kernel /tmp/lm.elf -d unimp,guest_errors -trace memory_region_ops_write -D /tmp/lm.log; echo rc=$?; wc -l &lt; /tmp/lm.log; grep -v '^$' /tmp/lm.log | head -n 20
timeout 5 qemu-system-arm -M lm3s6965evb -nographic -monitor none -serial null -kernel /tmp/lm.elf -icount shift=0 -singlestep -d nochain,exec -trace memory_region_ops_write -D /tmp/lm2.log; echo rc=$?; wc -l &lt; /tmp/lm2.log; grep -n 'memory_region_ops_write' /tmp/lm2.log | head -n 10
truncate -s 64M /tmp/sdsc.img &amp;&amp; truncate -s 4G /tmp/sdhc.img &amp;&amp; for i in sdsc sdhc; do timeout 3 qemu-system-arm -M lm3s6965evb -nographic -monitor none -serial null -kernel /tmp/lm.elf -drive if=sd,format=raw,file=/tmp/$i.img; echo $i rc=$?; done
timeout 3 qemu-system-arm -M lm3s6965evb -nographic -monitor none -serial null -kernel /tmp/lm.elf -drive if=sd,format=raw,file=/tmp/sdsc.img -global sd-card.spec_version=1; echo spec1 rc=$?
printf '.syntax unified\n.cpu cortex-m4\n.thumb\n.text\n.word 0x20001000\n.word reset\n.thumb_func\nreset:\nldr r0,=0x40023844\nldr r1,=0x100\nstr r1,[r0]\nldr r0,=0x40012304\nldr r1,=0x10000\nstr r1,[r0]\nldr r0,=0x40012008\nmovs r1,#1\nstr r1,[r0]\nldr r1,=0x40000001\nstr r1,[r0]\nldr r0,=0x40012000\nldr r2,[r0]\nldr r0,=0x4001204C\nldr r2,[r0]\nhalt:\nwfi\nb halt\n' &gt; /tmp/st.s &amp;&amp; arm-none-eabi-as -o /tmp/st.o /tmp/st.s &amp;&amp; arm-none-eabi-ld -Ttext=0x08000000 -o /tmp/st.elf /tmp/st.o &amp;&amp; timeout 5 qemu-system-arm -M netduinoplus2 -nographic -monitor none -serial null -kernel /tmp/st.elf -d unimp,guest_errors -trace 'memory_region_ops_*' -D /tmp/st.log; echo rc=$?; grep -v '^$' /tmp/st.log | head -n 20

/* lm3s6965evb: flash at 0x00000000, RAM at 0x20000000. */
MEMORY {
  FLASH (rx)  : ORIGIN = 0x00000000, LENGTH = 256K
  RAM   (rwx) : ORIGIN = 0x20000000, LENGTH = 64K
}
ENTRY(reset)
SECTIONS {
  .text : { KEEP(*(.vectors)) *(.text*) *(.rodata*) *(.ARM.exidx*) . = ALIGN(4); _etext = .; } &gt; FLASH
  .data : AT(_etext) { _sdata = .; *(.data*) . = ALIGN(4); _edata = .; } &gt; RAM
  .bss  : { _sbss = .; *(.bss*) *(COMMON) . = ALIGN(4); _ebss = .; } &gt; RAM
  _estack = ORIGIN(RAM) + LENGTH(RAM);
}

===== /app/data/run.sh
#!/bin/sh
# sh run.sh firmware.elf: the board, with its UART on stdin/stdout. mux=off so
# that a 0x01 byte on the wire is data and not a QEMU escape.
exec qemu-system-arm -M lm3s6965evb -nographic -monitor none \
    -chardev stdio,id=uart,mux=off,signal=off -serial chardev:uart -kernel "$1"

===== /app/data/start.c
/* Reset: copy .data from flash, zero .bss, call app_main. */
#include &lt;stdint.h&gt;

extern uint32_t _etext, _sdata, _edata, _sbss, _ebss, _estack;
void app_main(void);

void reset(void)
{
    uint32_t *s = &amp;_etext, *d = &amp;_sdata;
    while (d &lt; &amp;_edata) *d++ = *s++;
    for (d = &amp;_sbss; d &lt; &amp;_ebss;) *d++ = 0;
    app_main();
    for (;;) ;
}

__attribute__((section(".vectors"), used)) static void *vectors[] = { &amp;_estack, reset };

===== /app/data/uart.h
/* UART0 of the Stellaris LM3S6965, as QEMU's lm3s6965evb models it. */
#ifndef UART_H
#define UART_H
#include &lt;stdint.h&gt;

#define UART0_BASE   0x4000C000u
#define UART0_DR     (*(volatile uint32_t *)(UART0_BASE + 0x00))  /* data */
#define UART0_FR     (*(volatile uint32_t *)(UART0_BASE + 0x18))  /* flags */

#define UART_FR_RXFE 0x10u   /* receive FIFO empty */
#define UART_FR_TXFF 0x20u   /* transmit FIFO full */
#endif
